   4: import {
   5:   Breadcrumb,
   6:   CostBar,
   7:   DrillRow,

  10:   TIME_SCALES,

  14:   getTimeScaleSuffix,

  18: import {

  24: export default function CostSummaryCard({
  25:   people_cost_total,
  26:   business_cost_total,
  27:   asset_cost_total = 0,
  28:   total_asset_interest_annual = 0,
  29:   general_overheads_total = 0,
  30:   total_cost_burden,
  31:   total_recovery_hours = 0,
  32:   macro_required_operating_hour_rate = 0,
  33:   net_annual_business_open_hours = 0,
  34:   labour_detail = {},
  35:   asset_detail = {},
  36:   overhead_detail = {},
  37:   highlight_insight = "",
  38: }) {
  39:   const [timeScale, setTimeScale] = useState("hour");
  40:   const [activePath, setActivePath] = useState(["total"]);
  41:   const [hoveredItemKey, setHoveredItemKey] = useState("");
  42: 
  43:   const total_people_cost_annual = toNumber(people_cost_total);
  44:   const total_business_cost_annual = toNumber(business_cost_total);
  45:   const total_asset_cost_annual = toNumber(asset_cost_total);
  46:   const total_asset_interest = toNumber(total_asset_interest_annual);
  47:   const total_business_overheads = toNumber(general_overheads_total);
  48:   const total_cost_burden_annual = toNumber(total_cost_burden);
  49:   const recovery_hours_total = toNumber(total_recovery_hours);
  50: 
  51:   const hierarchy = useMemo(() => {
  52:     return buildCostSummaryHierarchy({
  53:       total_cost_burden_annual,
  54:       total_people_cost_annual,
  55:       total_asset_cost_annual,
  56:       total_asset_interest,
  57:       total_business_overheads,
  58:       labour_detail,
  59:       asset_detail,
  60:       overhead_detail,
  61:     });
  62:   }, [
  63:     total_cost_burden_annual,
  64:     total_people_cost_annual,
  65:     total_asset_cost_annual,
  66:     total_asset_interest,
  67:     total_business_overheads,
  68:     labour_detail,
  69:     asset_detail,
  70:     overhead_detail,
  71:   ]);
  72: 
  73:   const activeLevel = getNodeByPath(hierarchy, activePath);
  74:   const activeItems = activeLevel.items ?? [];
  75:   const breadcrumb = getBreadcrumbNodes(hierarchy, activePath);
  76: 
  77:   const headlineValue =
  78:     timeScale === "hour"
  79:       ? macro_required_operating_hour_rate
  80:       : timeScale === "year"
  81:         ? total_cost_burden_annual
  82:         : scaleAnnualValue(
  83:             total_cost_burden_annual,
  84:             timeScale,
  85:             recovery_hours_total,
  86:             net_annual_business_open_hours
  87:           );
  88: 
  89:   const insight =
  90:     highlight_insight ||
  91:     getInsightForLevel(
  92:       activeLevel.key,
  93:       activeItems,
  94:       total_cost_burden_annual
  95:     );
  96: 
  97:   function handleSelectLevel(item) {
  98:     setHoveredItemKey("");
  99:     setActivePath((currentPath) => [...currentPath, item.key]);
 100:   }
 101: 
 102:   function handleSelectBreadcrumb(index) {
 103:     setActivePath((currentPath) => currentPath.slice(0, index + 1));
 104:     setHoveredItemKey("");
 105:   }
 106: 
 107:   return (
 108:     <section className="ui-section">
 109:       <div className="ui-stack">
 110:         <div className="ui-panel ui-stack-sm">
 111:           <div className="ui-kicker">Cost Baseline</div>
 112:           <div className="ui-card-title">
 113:             What your business must recover
 114:           </div>
 115: 
 116:           <div className="cost-summary-hero">
 117:             <div className="ui-stack-sm">
 118:               <div className="ui-kicker">
 119:                 {timeScale === "hour" ? "Operating cost per open hour" : "Total cost burden"}
 120:               </div>
 121:               <div className="ui-display">
 122:                 {formatMoney(headlineValue)}
 123:               </div>
 124:               <div className="ui-help" style={{ fontSize: "0.9em", marginTop: "-4px" }}>
 125:                 {timeScale === "hour" ? "per open hr" : getTimeScaleSuffix(timeScale)}
 126:               </div>
 127:               <div className="ui-help" style={{ marginTop: "12px" }}>
 128:                 {timeScale === "hour"
 129:                   ? "Total business cost burden divided by net annual business open hours."
 130:                   : "This is the business cost baseline scaled to the selected period."}
 131:               </div>
 132:               {timeScale === "hour" && (
 133:                 <div className="ui-help" style={{ marginTop: "8px", fontSize: "0.85em" }}>
 134:                   Based on {formatNumber(net_annual_business_open_hours)} net annual business open hours.
 135:                 </div>
 136:               )}
 137:             </div>
 138: 
 139:             <div className="cost-summary-toggle" aria-label="Time scale">
 140:               {TIME_SCALES.map((option) => (
 141:                 <button
 142:                   key={option.key}
 143:                   type="button"
 144:                   className={
 145:                     option.key === timeScale
 146:                       ? "cost-summary-toggle-button active"
 147:                       : "cost-summary-toggle-button"
 148:                   }
 149:                   onClick={() => setTimeScale(option.key)}
 150:                 >
 151:                   {option.label}
 152:                 </button>
 153:               ))}
 154:             </div>
 155:           </div>
 156:         </div>
 157: 
 158:         <div className="ui-panel ui-stack-sm">
 159:           <Breadcrumb path={breadcrumb} onSelect={handleSelectBreadcrumb} />
 160: 
 161:           <div className="ui-split">
 162:             <div className="ui-stack-sm">
 163:               <div className="ui-kicker">Cost Composition</div>
 164:               <div className="ui-card-title-sm">{activeLevel.title}</div>
 165:               <div className="ui-help">
 166:                 {activeLevel.key === "people"
 167:                   ? "All staff costs are divided across the net annual business open hours."
 168:                   : "Click a section to explore. Values stay tied back to the total operating cost baseline."}
 169:               </div>
 170:             </div>
 171: 
 172:             <div className="cost-summary-level-total">
 173:               <span className="cost-summary-level-total-label">Total</span>
 174:               <span className="cost-summary-level-total-value">
 175:                 {formatMoney(
 176:                   scaleAnnualValue(
 177:                     activeLevel.total,
 178:                     timeScale,
 179:                     recovery_hours_total,
 180:                     net_annual_business_open_hours
 181:                   )
 182:                 )}
 183:                 <span className="cost-summary-level-total-suffix">
 184:                   {getTimeScaleSuffix(timeScale)}
 185:                 </span>
 186:               </span>
 187:             </div>
 188:           </div>
 189: 
 190:           <CostBar
 191:             items={activeItems}
 192:             total={activeLevel.total}
 193:             timeScale={timeScale}
 194:             totalRecoveryHours={recovery_hours_total}
 195:             openHours={net_annual_business_open_hours}
 196:             hoveredItemKey={hoveredItemKey}
 197:             onHoverItem={setHoveredItemKey}
 198:             onClearHover={() => setHoveredItemKey("")}
 199:             onSelect={handleSelectLevel}
 200:           />
 201: 
 202:           <div className="cost-summary-drill-list">
 203:             {activeItems.map((item) => (
 204:               <DrillRow
 205:                 key={item.key}
 206:                 item={item}
 207:                 parentTotal={activeLevel.total}
 208:                 total_cost_burden={total_cost_burden_annual}
 209:                 timeScale={timeScale}
 210:                 totalRecoveryHours={recovery_hours_total}
 211:                 openHours={net_annual_business_open_hours}
 212:                 hoveredItemKey={hoveredItemKey}
 213:                 onHoverItem={setHoveredItemKey}
 214:                 onClearHover={() => setHoveredItemKey("")}
 215:                 onSelect={handleSelectLevel}
 216:               />
 217:             ))}
 218:           </div>
 219: 
 220:           <div className="cost-summary-insight">
 221:             <div className="ui-kicker">Plain-language read</div>
 222:             <div className="ui-help">{insight}</div>
 223:           </div>
 224:         </div>
 225: 
 226:         <div className="ui-panel ui-stack-sm">
 227:           <div className="ui-kicker">Baseline Inputs</div>
 228:           <div className="ui-card-title-sm">What this is based on</div>
 229: 
 230:           <div className="labour-summary-table">
 231:             <div className="labour-summary-table-row">
 232:               <div className="labour-summary-table-label">
 233:                 Total Operating Cost
 234:               </div>
 235:               <div className="labour-summary-table-value">
 236:                 {formatMoney(total_cost_burden_annual)}
 237:               </div>
 238:             </div>
 239: 
 240:             <div className="labour-summary-table-row">
 241:               <div className="labour-summary-table-label">
 242:                 Net annual business open hours
 243:               </div>
 244:               <div className="labour-summary-table-value">
 245:                 {formatNumber(net_annual_business_open_hours)} hrs
 246:               </div>
 247:             </div>
 248: 
 249:             <div className="labour-summary-table-row total">
 250:               <div className="labour-summary-table-label">
 251:                 Operating cost per open hour
 252:               </div>
 253:               <div className="labour-summary-table-value">
 254:                 {formatMoney(macro_required_operating_hour_rate)} / open hr
 255:               </div>
 256:             </div>
 257:           </div>
 258: 
 259:           <div className="ui-help">
 260:             The selected period changes the display scale only. The baseline is
 261:             still built from total operating cost and net annual business open
 262:             hours. This is your break-even cost baseline. Materials and profit sit on
 263:             top.
 264:           </div>
 265:         </div>
 266:       </div>
 267:     </section>
 268:   );
 269: }
===== FILE: components\cost-summary\CostSummaryStatusStrip.jsx =====
   1: "use client";
   2: 
   3: function formatCurrency(value) {
   4:   const number = Number(value) || 0;
   5: 
   6:   return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-NZ", {
   7:     style: "currency",
   8:     currency: "NZD",
   9:     maximumFractionDigits: 0,
  10:   }).format(number);
  11: }
  12: 
  13: function formatHours(value) {
  14:   const number = Number(value) || 0;
  15: 
  16:   return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-NZ", {
  17:     maximumFractionDigits: 0,
  18:   }).format(number);
  19: }
  20: 
  21: function getLargestCostDriver({
  22:   total_people_cost_annual = 0,
  23:   total_asset_cost_annual = 0,
  24:   total_business_overheads = 0,
  25: }) {
  26:   const values = [
  27:     { label: "Labour", value: Number(total_people_cost_annual) || 0 },
  28:     { label: "Assets", value: Number(total_asset_cost_annual) || 0 },
  29:     { label: "General Overheads", value: Number(total_business_overheads) || 0 },
  30:   ];
  31: 
  32:   values.sort((a, b) => b.value - a.value);
  33: 
  34:   if ((values[0]?.value ?? 0) <= 0) {
  35:     return "";
  36:   }
  37: 
  38:   return `Largest cost driver: ${values[0].label}.`;
  39: }
  40: 
  41: export default function CostSummaryStatusStrip({
  42:   model_ready = false,
  43:   model_readiness_status = "blocked",
  44:   blocking_modules = [],
  45:   warning_modules = [],
  46:   blocking_checks = [],
  47:   warning_checks = [],
  48:   macro_required_operating_hour_rate = 0,
  49:   net_annual_business_open_hours = 0,
  50:   total_cost_burden = 0,
  51:   total_people_cost_annual = 0,
  52:   total_asset_cost_annual = 0,
  53:   total_business_overheads = 0,
  54: }) {
  55:   const macro_pressure_value = formatCurrency(
  56:     macro_required_operating_hour_rate
  57:   );
  58:   const macro_hours_value = Number(net_annual_business_open_hours || 0)
  59:     .toFixed(1)
  60:     .toString();
  61:   const insight = getLargestCostDriver({
  62:     total_people_cost_annual,
  63:     total_asset_cost_annual,
  64:     total_business_overheads,
  65:   });
  66:   const normalized_status = String(model_readiness_status || "").toLowerCase();
  67:   const status_title = !model_ready
  68:     ? "Cost Summary Not Trusted"
  69:     : normalized_status === "warning"
  70:       ? "Cost Summary Ready With Warnings"
  71:       : "Cost Summary Trusted";
  72:   const status_message = !model_ready
  73:     ? "Model Readiness is blocked. Fix the listed setup modules before treating Cost Summary as a trusted downstream baseline."
  74:     : normalized_status === "warning"
  75:       ? "The model is usable, but warning checks remain visible for review before downstream decisions."
  76:       : "The upstream cost setup is ready and this baseline can be used as the internal operating cost burden.";
  77: 
  78:   return (
  79:     <section className="ui-section">
  80:       <div className="ui-panel ui-stack-sm">
  81:         <div className="ui-kicker">Model Readiness</div>
  82: 
  83:         <div className="ui-card-title-sm">{status_title}</div>
  84: 
  85:         <p className="ui-help">{status_message}</p>
  86: 
  87:         {!model_ready && blocking_modules.length > 0 ? (
  88:           <p className="ui-help">
  89:             What is blocked: {blocking_modules.join(", ")}.
  90:           </p>
  91:         ) : null}
  92: 
  93:         {!model_ready && blocking_checks.length > 0 ? (
  94:           <p className="ui-help">
  95:             Where to fix it: return to the blocked setup modules and clear the
  96:             Model Readiness checks.
  97:           </p>
  98:         ) : null}
  99: 
 100:         {!model_ready ? (
 101:           <p className="ui-help">
 102:             If ignored: this rate remains a preview only and should not be used
 103:             for downstream decisions.
 104:           </p>
 105:         ) : null}
 106: 
 107:         {warning_modules.length > 0 ? (
 108:           <p className="ui-help">
 109:             Warning modules: {warning_modules.join(", ")}.
 110:           </p>
 111:         ) : null}
 112: 
 113:         {warning_checks.length > 0 ? (
 114:           <p className="ui-help">
 115:             Warning checks: {warning_checks.length}. Review before relying on
 116:             downstream decisions.
 117:           </p>
 118:         ) : null}
 119: 
 120:         <div className="ui-kicker">Operating Cost Baseline</div>
 121: 
 122:         <div className="ui-card-title-sm">
 123:           {macro_pressure_value} / open hr
 124:         </div>
 125: 
 126:         <p className="ui-help">
 127:           Total business cost burden divided by net annual business open hours.
 128:         </p>
 129: 
 130:         <p className="ui-help">Based on {macro_hours_value} net annual business open hours.</p>
 131: 
 132:         {!model_ready ? (
 133:           <p className="ui-help">
 134:             This rate is not trusted until Model Readiness passes.
 135:           </p>
 136:         ) : null}
 137: 
 138:         {insight ? <p className="ui-help">{insight}</p> : null}
 139:       </div>
 140:     </section>
 141:   );
 142: }
===== FILE: components\cost-summary\cost-summary-card\costSummaryHierarchy.js =====
   1: import { toNumber } from "@/components/cost-summary/cost-summary-card/costSummaryFormatters";
   2: 
   3: function makeNode({ key, label, amount = 0, title, note, children = [] }) {
   4:   return {
   5:     key,
   6:     label,
   7:     title: title || label,
   8:     amount: toNumber(amount),
   9:     total: toNumber(amount),
  10:     note,
  11:     children,
  12:     items: children,
  13:   };
  14: }
  15: 
  16: export function getNodeByPath(root, activePath = []) {
  17:   return activePath.slice(1).reduce((node, key) => {
  18:     return node?.children?.find((child) => child.key === key) ?? node;
  19:   }, root);
  20: }
  21: 
  22: export function getBreadcrumbNodes(root, activePath = []) {
  23:   const nodes = [root];
  24:   let node = root;
  25: 
  26:   for (const key of activePath.slice(1)) {
  27:     const next = node?.children?.find((child) => child.key === key);
  28:     if (!next) break;
  29:     nodes.push(next);
  30:     node = next;
  31:   }
  32: 
  33:   return nodes;
  34: }
  35: 
  36: export function buildCostSummaryHierarchy({
  37:   total_cost_burden_annual,
  38:   total_people_cost_annual,
  39:   total_asset_cost_annual,
  40:   total_asset_interest,
  41:   total_business_overheads,
  42:   labour_detail,
  43:   asset_detail,
  44:   overhead_detail,
  45: }) {
  46:   function buildContributionNodes(prefix, detail = {}) {
  47:     return [
  48:       makeNode({
  49:         key: `${prefix}-kiwisaver`,
  50:         label: "KiwiSaver",
  51:         amount: detail.employer_kiwisaver_annual,
  52:       }),
  53:       makeNode({
  54:         key: `${prefix}-esct`,
  55:         label: "ESCT",
  56:         amount: detail.esct_annual,
  57:       }),
  58:       makeNode({
  59:         key: `${prefix}-acc`,
  60:         label: "ACC",
  61:         amount: detail.acc_levy_annual,
  62:       }),
  63:     ];
  64:   }
  65: 
  66:   function buildEntitlementNodes(prefix, detail = {}) {
  67:     return [
  68:       makeNode({
  69:         key: `${prefix}-annual-leave`,
  70:         label: "Annual Leave",
  71:         amount: detail.annual_leave_cost_annual,
  72:       }),
  73:       makeNode({
  74:         key: `${prefix}-public-holidays`,
  75:         label: "Public Holidays",
  76:         amount: detail.public_holiday_cost_annual,
  77:       }),
  78:       makeNode({
  79:         key: `${prefix}-sick-leave`,
  80:         label: "Sick Leave",
  81:         amount: detail.sick_leave_cost_annual,
  82:       }),
  83:       makeNode({
  84:         key: `${prefix}-bereavement-leave`,
  85:         label: "Bereavement Leave",
  86:         amount: detail.bereavement_leave_cost_annual,
  87:       }),
  88:       makeNode({
  89:         key: `${prefix}-family-violence-leave`,
  90:         label: "Family Violence Leave",
  91:         amount: detail.family_violence_leave_cost_annual,
  92:       }),
  93:     ];
  94:   }
  95: 
  96:   function buildLabourClassNode(key, label, detail = {}) {
  97:     return makeNode({
  98:       key,
  99:       label,
 100:       amount: detail.total_labour_cost_annual,
 101:       children: [
 102:         makeNode({
 103:           key: `${key}-base-wages`,
 104:           label: "Base Wages",
 105:           amount: detail.base_wages_annual,
 106:         }),
 107:         makeNode({
 108:           key: `${key}-employer-contributions`,
 109:           label: "Employer Contributions",
 110:           amount: detail.employer_contribution_annual,
 111:           children: buildContributionNodes(key, detail),
 112:         }),
 113:         makeNode({
 114:           key: `${key}-entitlements`,
 115:           label: "Entitlements",
 116:           amount: detail.entitlements_annual,
 117:           note: "Entitlement cost is separated from base wages here for review.",
 118:           children: buildEntitlementNodes(key, detail),
 119:         }),
 120:       ],
 121:     });
 122:   }
 123: 
 124:   const running_cost_annual = toNumber(asset_detail.running_cost_annual);
 125:   const asset_children = [
 126:     makeNode({
 127:       key: "asset-total-cost",
 128:       label: "Total Asset Cost",
 129:       amount: total_asset_cost_annual,
 130:     }),
 131:     makeNode({
 132:       key: "asset-finance-interest",
 133:       label: "Asset Finance Interest",
 134:       amount: total_asset_interest,
 135:       note: "Supporting detail only. This is already included inside Asset Cost where applicable.",
 136:     }),
 137:   ];
 138: 
 139:   if (running_cost_annual > 0) {
 140:     asset_children.push(
 141:       makeNode({
 142:         key: "asset-running-cost",
 143:         label: "Running Cost",
 144:         amount: running_cost_annual,
 145:         note: "Legacy display only where upstream Assets exposes running cost.",
 146:       })
 147:     );
 148:   }
 149: 
 150:   const category_totals = Array.isArray(overhead_detail.category_totals)
 151:     ? overhead_detail.category_totals
 152:     : [];
 153:   const overhead_children = category_totals
 154:     .filter((category) => toNumber(category?.total) > 0)
 155:     .map((category) =>
 156:       makeNode({
 157:         key: `overheads-${category.category_id}`,
 158:         label: category.category_name || category.category_id,
 159:         amount: category.total,
 160:       })
 161:     );
 162: 
 163:   if (overhead_children.length === 0) {
 164:     overhead_children.push(
 165:       makeNode({
 166:         key: "general-overheads-total",
 167:         label: "Total General Overheads",
 168:         amount: total_business_overheads,
 169:         note: "Future-ready: category-level overhead outputs are not available in this Cost Summary view.",
 170:       })
 171:     );
 172:   }
 173: 
 174:   return makeNode({
 175:     key: "total",
 176:     label: "Total Cost",
 177:     title: "Where your business cost comes from",
 178:     amount: total_cost_burden_annual,
 179:     children: [
 180:       makeNode({
 181:         key: "people",
 182:         label: "People Cost",
 183:         title: "People cost breakdown",
 184:         amount: total_people_cost_annual,
 185:         children: [
 186:           buildLabourClassNode(
 187:             "productive-labour",
 188:             "Productive Labour",
 189:             labour_detail.productive
 190:           ),
 191:           buildLabourClassNode(
 192:             "non-productive-labour",
 193:             "Non-productive Labour",
 194:             labour_detail.non_productive
 195:           ),
 196:         ],
 197:       }),
 198:       makeNode({
 199:         key: "assets",
 200:         label: "Assets",
 201:         title: "Asset cost breakdown",
 202:         amount: total_asset_cost_annual,
 203:         children: asset_children,
 204:       }),
 205:       makeNode({
 206:         key: "overheads",
 207:         label: "General Overheads",
 208:         title: "General overhead breakdown",
 209:         amount: total_business_overheads,
 210:         children: overhead_children,
 211:       }),
 212:     ],
 213:   });
 214: }
===== FILE: components\cost-summary\cost-summary-card\CostSummaryDrill.jsx =====
   1: import { formatCostSummaryPercent } from "@/lib/selectors/costSummarySelectors";
   2: import {
   3:   calculateShare,
   4:   formatMoney,
   5:   getTimeScaleSuffix,
   6:   scaleAnnualValue,
   7:   toNumber,
   8: } from "@/components/cost-summary/cost-summary-card/costSummaryFormatters";
   9: 
  10: export function CostBar({
  11:   items,
  12:   total,
  13:   timeScale,
  14:   totalRecoveryHours,
  15:   openHours = 0,
  16:   hoveredItemKey,
  17:   onHoverItem,
  18:   onClearHover,
  19:   onSelect,
  20: }) {
  21:   const safeTotal = toNumber(total);
  22:   const hasPositiveTotal = safeTotal > 0;
  23:   const hasPositiveSegments = items.some((item) => toNumber(item.amount) > 0);
  24: 
  25:   if (!hasPositiveTotal || !hasPositiveSegments || items.length === 0) {
  26:     return (
  27:       <div
  28:         className="cost-summary-bar empty"
  29:         aria-label="Cost composition unavailable"
  30:       >
  31:         <div className="cost-summary-bar-empty" />
  32:       </div>
  33:     );
  34:   }
  35: 
  36:   return (
  37:     <div className="cost-summary-bar" aria-label="Cost composition">
  38:       {items.map((item, index) => {
  39:         const hasChildren =
  40:           Array.isArray(item.children) && item.children.length > 0;
  41:         const share = calculateShare(item.amount, safeTotal);
  42:         const scaledValue = scaleAnnualValue(
  43:           item.amount,
  44:           timeScale,
  45:           totalRecoveryHours,
  46:           openHours
  47:         );
  48:         const isActive = hoveredItemKey === item.key;
  49:         const isMuted = Boolean(hoveredItemKey) && !isActive;
  50:         const className = [
  51:           "cost-summary-bar-segment",
  52:           `cost-summary-bar-segment--${index % 6}`,
  53:           hasChildren ? "clickable" : "static",
  54:           isActive ? "active" : "",
  55:           isMuted ? "muted" : "",
  56:         ]
  57:           .filter(Boolean)
  58:           .join(" ");
  59:         const sharedProps = {
  60:           className,
  61:           style: { "--segment-grow": `${Math.max(toNumber(item.amount), 0)}` },
  62:           onMouseEnter: () => onHoverItem(item.key),
  63:           onMouseLeave: onClearHover,
  64:           "aria-label": `${item.label}: ${formatMoney(
  65:             scaledValue
  66:           )}${getTimeScaleSuffix(timeScale)}, ${formatCostSummaryPercent(
  67:             share
  68:           )} of this layer`,
  69:           title: `${item.label}: ${formatMoney(
  70:             scaledValue
  71:           )}${getTimeScaleSuffix(timeScale)}, ${formatCostSummaryPercent(
  72:             share
  73:           )} of this layer`,
  74:         };
  75: 
  76:         if (!hasChildren) {
  77:           return <div key={item.key} role="img" {...sharedProps} />;
  78:         }
  79: 
  80:         return (
  81:           <button
  82:             key={item.key}
  83:             type="button"
  84:             {...sharedProps}
  85:             onFocus={() => onHoverItem(item.key)}
  86:             onBlur={onClearHover}
  87:             onClick={() => onSelect(item)}
  88:           />
  89:         );
  90:       })}
  91:     </div>
  92:   );
  93: }
  94: 
  95: export function DrillRow({
  96:   item,
  97:   parentTotal,
  98:   total_cost_burden,
  99:   timeScale,
 100:   totalRecoveryHours,
 101:   openHours = 0,
 102:   hoveredItemKey,
 103:   onHoverItem,
 104:   onClearHover,
 105:   onSelect,
 106: }) {
 107:   const hasChildren = Array.isArray(item.children) && item.children.length > 0;
 108:   const scaledValue = scaleAnnualValue(
 109:     item.amount,
 110:     timeScale,
 111:     totalRecoveryHours,
 112:     openHours
 113:   );
 114: 
 115:   const shareOfParent = calculateShare(item.amount, parentTotal);
 116:   const shareOfTotal = calculateShare(item.amount, total_cost_burden);
 117:   const isActive = hoveredItemKey === item.key;
 118:   const isMuted = Boolean(hoveredItemKey) && !isActive;
 119:   const className = [
 120:     "cost-summary-drill-row",
 121:     hasChildren ? "clickable" : "static",
 122:     isActive ? "active" : "",
 123:     isMuted ? "muted" : "",
 124:   ]
 125:     .filter(Boolean)
 126:     .join(" ");
 127: 
 128:   const content = (
 129:     <>
 130:       <div className="ui-stack-sm">
 131:         <div className="cost-summary-drill-label">{item.label}</div>
 132:         {item.note ? <div className="ui-help">{item.note}</div> : null}
 133:       </div>
 134: 
 135:       <div className="cost-summary-drill-value">
 136:         <div className="ui-card-title-sm">
 137:           {formatMoney(scaledValue)}
 138:           <span className="ui-help"> {getTimeScaleSuffix(timeScale)}</span>
 139:         </div>
 140:         <div className="ui-help">
 141:           {formatCostSummaryPercent(shareOfParent)} of this layer
 142:           {" · "}
 143:           {formatCostSummaryPercent(shareOfTotal)} of total
 144:         </div>
 145:       </div>
 146:     </>
 147:   );
 148: 
 149:   const sharedProps = {
 150:     className,
 151:     onMouseEnter: () => onHoverItem(item.key),
 152:     onMouseLeave: onClearHover,
 153:   };
 154: 
 155:   if (!hasChildren) {
 156:     return <div {...sharedProps}>{content}</div>;
 157:   }
 158: 
 159:   return (
 160:     <button
 161:       type="button"
 162:       {...sharedProps}
 163:       onFocus={() => onHoverItem(item.key)}
 164:       onBlur={onClearHover}
 165:       onClick={() => onSelect(item)}
 166:     >
 167:       {content}
 168:     </button>
 169:   );
 170: }
 171: 
 172: export function Breadcrumb({ path, onSelect }) {
 173:   return (
 174:     <div className="cost-summary-breadcrumb">
 175:       {path.map((crumb, index) => (
 176:         <button
 177:           key={`${crumb.key}-${index}`}
 178:           type="button"
 179:           className="cost-summary-breadcrumb-item"
 180:           onClick={() => onSelect(index)}
 181:         >
 182:           {crumb.label}
 183:         </button>
 184:       ))}
 185:     </div>
 186:   );
 187: }

  55:             : ` ${result_table.scale_suffix}`}
  56:         </span>
  57:       </div>
  58: 
  59:       <p className="ui-help">
  60:         {product_mode_active
  61:           ? hero.product_helper_text
  62:           : hero.recovery_headline}
  63:       </p>
  64:

  66:         <p className="ui-help">{hero.product_secondary_result}</p>
  67:       ) : (
  68:         <>
  69:           <p className="ui-help">
  70:             Owner wages are already included in costs, so this result is after
  71:             paying the owner properly.
  72:           </p>
  73:           <p className="ui-help">
  74:             This compares your actual Gross Profit / Margin Pool against the
  75:             Cost Summary operating cost baseline.
  76:           </p>

  92:             {option.label}

  94:         ))}

  15: 
  16: function MacroRow({
  17:   id,
  18:   title,
  19:   help,
  20:   value,
  21:   active,
  22:   total = false,
  23:   onClick,
  24: }) {

  26:

  28:     active ? "is-active" : ""
  29:   } ${total ? "total" : ""}`;
  30: 
  31:   if (!is_clickable) {
  32:     return (
  33:       <div className={class_name}>
  34:         <div className="business-summary-macro-row-label">
  35:           <div className="business-summary-macro-row-title">{title}</div>

  37:             <div className="business-summary-macro-row-help">{help}</div>
  38:           ) : null}
  39:         </div>
  40: 
  41:         <div className="business-summary-macro-row-value">{value}</div>
  42:       </div>
  43:     );
  44:   }
  45: 
  46:   return (
  47:     <button
  48:       type="button"
  49:       onClick={() => onClick(active ? null : id)}
  50:       className={class_name}
  51:     >
  52:       <div className="business-summary-macro-row-label">
  53:         <div className="business-summary-macro-row-title">{title}</div>
  54:         {help ? (
  55:           <div className="business-summary-macro-row-help">{help}</div>
  56:         ) : null}
  57:       </div>
  58: 
  59:       <div className="business-summary-macro-row-value">{value}</div>
  60:     </button>
  61:   );

  82:           {Math.abs(to_number(variance)) < 1 ? "Reconciled" : "Review"}

 173: function BridgeReconciliationProof({
 174:   pnl_net_profit = 0,
 175:   labour_variance = 0,
 176:   asset_finance_variance = 0,
 177:   overhead_variance = 0,
 178:   other_income_treatment = 0,
 179:   qs_tools_operating_position = 0,
 180: }) {
 181:   return (
 182:     <div className="ui-panel ui-stack-sm">
 183:       <div>
 184:         <div className="ui-kicker">Reconciliation proof</div>
 185:         <p className="ui-help">
 186:           This confirms how traditional P&amp;L net profit bridges to the QS
 187:           Tools operating position. Cost-burden movements and Other Income
 188:           treatment are shown separately so income and expenses are not treated
 189:           as one category.
 190:         </p>
 191:       </div>
 192: 
 193:       <div className="labour-summary-table">
 194:         <BridgeProofRow
 195:           label="Traditional P&L Net Profit"
 196:           value={pnl_net_profit}
 197:         />
 198: 
 199:         <BridgeProofRow
 200:           label="Less labour variance"
 201:           value={-Math.abs(to_number(labour_variance))}
 202:         />
 203: 
 204:         <BridgeProofRow
 205:           label="Less asset finance variance"
 206:           value={-Math.abs(to_number(asset_finance_variance))}
 207:         />
 208: 
 209:         <BridgeProofRow
 210:           label="Less overhead variance"
 211:           value={-Math.abs(to_number(overhead_variance))}
 212:         />
 213: 
 214:         <BridgeProofRow
 215:           label="Less Other Income excluded"
 216:           value={-Math.abs(to_number(other_income_treatment))}
 217:         />
 218: 
 219:         <BridgeProofRow
 220:           label="QS Tools Operating Position"
 221:           value={qs_tools_operating_position}
 222:           is_total
 223:         />
 224:       </div>
 225:     </div>
 226:   );
 227: }

 229: function OperationalRealityBridge({
 230:   labour_benchmark_total = 0,
 231:   total_people_cost_annual = 0,
 232: 
 233:   asset_finance_benchmark_total = 0,
 234:   total_asset_interest_annual = 0,
 235: 
 236:   general_overheads_benchmark_total = 0,
 237:   total_business_overheads = 0,
 238: 
 239:   other_income = 0,
 240:   pnl_net_profit = 0,
 241:   qs_tools_operating_position = 0,
 242: }) {
 243:   const labour_variance =
 244:     to_number(total_people_cost_annual) - to_number(labour_benchmark_total);
 245: 
 246:   const asset_finance_variance =
 247:     to_number(total_asset_interest_annual) -
 248:     to_number(asset_finance_benchmark_total);
 249: 
 250:   const adjusted_overheads_benchmark =
 251:     to_number(general_overheads_benchmark_total) -
 252:     to_number(asset_finance_benchmark_total);
 253: 
 254:   const overhead_variance =
 255:     to_number(total_business_overheads) - adjusted_overheads_benchmark;
 256: 
 257:   const operational_pressure_adjustment =
 258:     labour_variance + asset_finance_variance + overhead_variance;
 259: 
 260:   const other_income_treatment = to_number(other_income);
 261: 
 262:   const total_bridge_adjustment =
 263:     operational_pressure_adjustment + other_income_treatment;
 264: 
 265:   return (
 266:     <div className="ui-panel ui-stack">
 267:       <div>
 268:         <p className="ui-kicker">P&amp;L to Operational Reality Bridge</p>
 269: 
 270:         <h3 className="ui-card-title-sm text-[var(--text-primary)]">
 271:           Why this position differs from the P&amp;L benchmark
 272:         </h3>
 273: 
 274:         <p className="ui-help">
 275:           The P&amp;L shows the historical accounting position. QS Tools shows
 276:           the operating burden the business is currently carrying. This bridge
 277:           explains the difference before downstream recovery and outcome
 278:           decisions are trusted.
 279:         </p>
 280:       </div>
 281: 
 282:       <div className="ui-stack-sm">
 283:         <BridgeRow
 284:           title="Labour variance"
 285:           help="Labour variance is a review signal, not a conclusion. It may reflect current pay assumptions, staff mix, timing, classification differences, or actual labour movement."
 286:           benchmark={labour_benchmark_total}
 287:           module_total={total_people_cost_annual}
 288:           variance={labour_variance}
 289:           benchmark_label="P&L labour benchmark"
 290:           module_label="People Cost from Cost Summary"
 291:         />
 292: 
 293:         <BridgeRow
 294:           title="Asset finance variance"
 295:           help="This highlights asset finance / ownership cost that may not yet be fully visible in the P&L until interest, depreciation, or journal timing is complete."
 296:           benchmark={asset_finance_benchmark_total}
 297:           module_total={total_asset_interest_annual}
 298:           variance={asset_finance_variance}
 299:           benchmark_label="P&L interest marked as asset finance"
 300:           module_label="Assets finance / interest cost"
 301:         />
 302: 
 303:         <BridgeRow
 304:           title="General overheads reconciliation"
 305:           help="General Overheads is checked after asset finance interest is removed from the P&L overhead benchmark, because asset finance is reviewed separately against Assets."
 306:           benchmark={adjusted_overheads_benchmark}
 307:           module_total={total_business_overheads}
 308:           variance={overhead_variance}
 309:           benchmark_label="Adjusted P&L overhead benchmark"
 310:           module_label="Net General Overheads"
 311:         />
 312: 
 313:         <OtherIncomeBridgeRow other_income={other_income_treatment} />
 314:       </div>
 315: 
 316:       <div className="business-summary-macro-row total">
 317:         <div className="business-summary-macro-row-label">
 318:           <div className="business-summary-macro-row-title">
 319:             Total difference to P&amp;L net profit
 320:           </div>
 321:           <div className="business-summary-macro-row-help">
 322:             Cost-burden movements and Other Income treatment explain why the QS
 323:             Tools operating position differs from traditional P&amp;L net
 324:             profit.
 325:           </div>
 326:         </div>
 327: 
 328:         <div className="business-summary-macro-row-value">
 329:           {format_currency(total_bridge_adjustment)}
 330:         </div>
 331:       </div>
 332: 
 333:       <BridgeReconciliationProof
 334:         pnl_net_profit={pnl_net_profit}
 335:         labour_variance={labour_variance}
 336:         asset_finance_variance={asset_finance_variance}
 337:         overhead_variance={overhead_variance}
 338:         other_income_treatment={other_income_treatment}
 339:         qs_tools_operating_position={qs_tools_operating_position}
 340:       />
 341:     </div>
 342:   );
 343: }

===== FILE: components\business-summary\BusinessSummaryStatusStrip.jsx =====
   1: "use client";
   2: 
   3: function formatCurrency(value) {
   4:   const number = Number(value) || 0;
   5: 
   6:   return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-NZ", {
   7:     style: "currency",
   8:     currency: "NZD",
   9:     maximumFractionDigits: 0,
  10:   }).format(number);
  11: }
  12: 
  13: export default function BusinessSummaryStatusStrip({
  14:   net_position = 0,
  15:   macro_required_operating_hour_rate = 0,
  16:   net_annual_business_open_hours = 0,
  17:   warning_count = 0,
  18:   business_summary_warnings = [],
  19: }) {
  20:   const macro_val = formatCurrency(macro_required_operating_hour_rate);
  21:   const macro_hours = Number(net_annual_business_open_hours || 0).toFixed(1);
  22: 
  23:   return (
  24:     <section className="ui-section">

  29:           Read-only comparison of trading output and operating cost burden.

  32:         <div className="labour-summary-table">
  33:           <div className="labour-summary-table-row total">
  34:             <div className="labour-summary-table-label">Net Position</div>
  35:             <div className="labour-summary-table-value">
  36:               {formatCurrency(net_position)}
  37:             </div>
  38:           </div>
  39: 
  40:           <div className="labour-summary-table-row">
  41:             <div className="labour-summary-table-label">
  42:               Required operating cost per open hour
  43:             </div>
  44:             <div className="labour-summary-table-value">
  45:               {macro_val} $/open hr
  46:             </div>
  47:           </div>
  48: 
  49:           <div className="labour-summary-table-row">
  50:             <div className="labour-summary-table-label">Based on</div>
  51:             <div className="labour-summary-table-value">
  52:               {macro_hours} net annual business open hours
  53:             </div>
  54:           </div>
  55: 
  56:           <div style={{ height: 8 }} />
  57: 
  58:           <div className="labour-summary-table-row">
  59:             <div className="labour-summary-table-label">Warnings</div>
  60:             <div className="labour-summary-table-value">{warning_count}</div>
  61:           </div>
  62:         </div>

  67:               <p className="ui-help" key={warning.warning_id}>
  68:                 {warning.message}
  69:               </p>
  70:             ))}
  71:           </div>
  72:         ) : null}
  73:       </div>
  74:     </section>

   7: function CalculationRow({ row, active, onClick, values }) {
   8:   const is_clickable = Boolean(row.drilldown_key);
   9: 
  10:   return (
  11:     <div className="ui-stack-sm">
  12:       {is_clickable ? (
  13:         <button
  14:           type="button"
  15:           onClick={() => onClick(active ? null : row.drilldown_key)}
  16:           className={`business-summary-macro-row ${
  17:             row.emphasis ? "total" : ""
  18:           } ${active ? "is-active" : ""}`}
  19:         >
  20:           <div className="business-summary-macro-row-label">
  21:             <div className="business-summary-macro-row-title">
  22:               {row.label}
  23:             </div>
  24:           </div>
  25: 
  26:           <div className="business-summary-macro-row-value">
  27:             {row.value}
  28:           </div>
  29:         </button>
  30:       ) : (
  31:         <div
  32:           className={`business-summary-macro-row ${
  33:             row.emphasis ? "total" : ""
  34:           }`}
  35:         >
  36:           <div className="business-summary-macro-row-label">
  37:             <div className="business-summary-macro-row-title">
  38:               {row.label}
  39:             </div>
  40:           </div>
  41: 
  42:           <div className="business-summary-macro-row-value">
  43:             {row.value}
  44:           </div>
  45:         </div>
  46:       )}
  47: 
  48:       {active ? (
  49:         <BusinessSummarySourceBreakdown
  50:           active_breakdown={row.drilldown_key}
  51:           values={values}
  52:         />
  53:       ) : null}
  54:     </div>
  55:   );
  56: }
  57: 
  58: export default function BusinessSummaryCalculationTable({
  59:   title,
  60:   rows = [],
  61:   formula,
  62:   values,
  63:   active_breakdown,
  64:   on_active_breakdown_change,
  65: }) {
  66:   const [local_active_breakdown, set_local_active_breakdown] = useState(null);
  67: 
  68:   const controlled = typeof on_active_breakdown_change === "function";
  69:   const current_active = controlled ? active_breakdown : local_active_breakdown;
  70:   const set_current_active = controlled
  71:     ? on_active_breakdown_change
  72:     : set_local_active_breakdown;
  73: 
  74:   return (
  75:     <div className="ui-stack-sm">
  76:       <p className="business-summary-macro-section-title">{title}</p>
  77: 
  78:       <div className="ui-stack-sm">
  79:         {rows.map((row) => (
  80:           <CalculationRow
  81:             key={row.label}
  82:             row={row}
  83:             active={current_active === row.drilldown_key}
  84:             onClick={set_current_active}
  85:             values={values}
  86:           />
  87:         ))}

===== FILE: hooks\cost-allocation\useCostAllocationAssetOverlay.js =====
   1: "use client";
   2: 
   3: import { useMemo } from "react";
   4: 
   5: import {
   6:   safe_array,
   7:   safe_number,
   8: } from "@/lib/calculations/cost-allocation/costAllocationInputBuilder";
   9: 
  10: function normalise_asset_type(value) {
  11:   return value === "support" ? "support" : "productive";
  12: }
  13: 
  14: function get_asset_name(asset = {}) {
  15:   return asset.asset_name || asset.name || "Unnamed Asset";
  16: }
  17: 
  18: function get_asset_recovery_hours(asset = {}, default_recovery_hours = 0) {
  19:   return (
  20:     safe_number(asset.utilisation_hours_annual) ||
  21:     safe_number(asset.asset_utilisation_hours_annual) ||
  22:     safe_number(asset.recovery_hours_annual) ||
  23:     safe_number(asset.asset_recovery_hours_used) ||
  24:     safe_number(default_recovery_hours)
  25:   );
  26: }
  27: 
  28: function build_asset_recovery_overlay({
  29:   asset_output_contract = {},
  30:   recovery_hours_used = 0,
  31: }) {
  32:   const active_assets = safe_array(asset_output_contract.active_assets);
  33:   const default_recovery_hours = safe_number(recovery_hours_used);
  34: 
  35:   const recovery_assets = active_assets.map((asset) => {
  36:     const base_asset_cost_annual = safe_number(asset.total_asset_cost_annual);
  37:     const allocated_asset_overhead_cost_annual = safe_number(
  38:       asset.allocated_asset_overhead_cost_annual
  39:     );
  40: 
  41:     const asset_recovery_cost_annual =
  42:       asset.asset_recovery_cost_annual !== undefined
  43:         ? safe_number(asset.asset_recovery_cost_annual)
  44:         : base_asset_cost_annual + allocated_asset_overhead_cost_annual;
  45: 
  46:     const asset_recovery_hours_used = get_asset_recovery_hours(
  47:       asset,
  48:       default_recovery_hours
  49:     );
  50: 
  51:     const asset_base_recovery_rate_per_hour =
  52:       asset_recovery_hours_used > 0
  53:         ? base_asset_cost_annual / asset_recovery_hours_used
  54:         : 0;
  55: 
  56:     const allocated_asset_overhead_recovery_rate =
  57:       asset_recovery_hours_used > 0
  58:         ? allocated_asset_overhead_cost_annual / asset_recovery_hours_used
  59:         : 0;
  60: 
  61:     const asset_recovery_rate_per_hour =
  62:       asset_recovery_hours_used > 0
  63:         ? asset_recovery_cost_annual / asset_recovery_hours_used
  64:         : 0;
  65: 
  66:     return {
  67:       ...asset,
  68:       asset_type: normalise_asset_type(asset.asset_type),
  69:       asset_name: get_asset_name(asset),
  70: 
  71:       base_asset_cost_annual,
  72:       allocated_asset_overhead_cost_annual,
  73:       asset_recovery_cost_annual,
  74: 
  75:       utilisation_hours_annual: asset_recovery_hours_used,
  76:       asset_recovery_hours_used,
  77: 
  78:       asset_base_recovery_rate_per_hour,
  79:       allocated_asset_overhead_recovery_rate,
  80:       asset_recovery_rate_per_hour,
  81: 
  82:       minimum_recoverable_asset_rate: asset_recovery_rate_per_hour,
  83:       fully_loaded_asset_recovery_rate: asset_recovery_rate_per_hour,
  84: 
  85:       cost_allocation_asset_cost_annual: asset_recovery_cost_annual,
  86:     };
  87:   });
  88: 
  89:   const asset_recovery_rows = recovery_assets.map((asset) => ({
  90:     asset_id: asset.asset_id ?? "",
  91:     asset_name: asset.asset_name ?? "Unnamed Asset",
  92:     asset_type: asset.asset_type,
  93: 
  94:     base_asset_cost_annual: safe_number(asset.base_asset_cost_annual),
  95:     allocated_asset_overhead_cost_annual: safe_number(
  96:       asset.allocated_asset_overhead_cost_annual
  97:     ),
  98:     asset_recovery_cost_annual: safe_number(asset.asset_recovery_cost_annual),
  99: 
 100:     utilisation_hours_annual: safe_number(asset.utilisation_hours_annual),
 101:     asset_recovery_hours_used: safe_number(asset.asset_recovery_hours_used),
 102: 
 103:     asset_base_recovery_rate_per_hour: safe_number(
 104:       asset.asset_base_recovery_rate_per_hour
 105:     ),
 106:     allocated_asset_overhead_recovery_rate: safe_number(
 107:       asset.allocated_asset_overhead_recovery_rate
 108:     ),
 109:     asset_recovery_rate_per_hour: safe_number(
 110:       asset.asset_recovery_rate_per_hour
 111:     ),
 112: 
 113:     minimum_recoverable_asset_rate: safe_number(
 114:       asset.minimum_recoverable_asset_rate
 115:     ),
 116:     fully_loaded_asset_recovery_rate: safe_number(
 117:       asset.fully_loaded_asset_recovery_rate
 118:     ),
 119:   }));
 120: 
 121:   const productive_assets = recovery_assets.filter(
 122:     (asset) => asset.asset_type === "productive"
 123:   );
 124: 
 125:   const support_assets = recovery_assets.filter(
 126:     (asset) => asset.asset_type === "support"
 127:   );
 128: 
 129:   const productive_asset_base_cost = productive_assets.reduce(
 130:     (sum, asset) => sum + safe_number(asset.base_asset_cost_annual),
 131:     0
 132:   );
 133: 
 134:   const support_asset_base_cost = support_assets.reduce(
 135:     (sum, asset) => sum + safe_number(asset.base_asset_cost_annual),
 136:     0
 137:   );
 138: 
 139:   const productive_asset_allocated_overhead_cost = productive_assets.reduce(
 140:     (sum, asset) =>
 141:       sum + safe_number(asset.allocated_asset_overhead_cost_annual),
 142:     0
 143:   );
 144: 
 145:   const support_asset_allocated_overhead_cost = support_assets.reduce(
 146:     (sum, asset) =>
 147:       sum + safe_number(asset.allocated_asset_overhead_cost_annual),
 148:     0
 149:   );
 150: 
 151:   const productive_asset_recovery_cost = productive_assets.reduce(
 152:     (sum, asset) => sum + safe_number(asset.asset_recovery_cost_annual),
 153:     0
 154:   );
 155: 
 156:   const support_asset_recovery_cost = support_assets.reduce(
 157:     (sum, asset) => sum + safe_number(asset.asset_recovery_cost_annual),
 158:     0
 159:   );
 160: 
 161:   return {
 162:     active_assets: recovery_assets,
 163:     asset_recovery_rows,
 164: 
 165:     productive_asset_base_cost,
 166:     support_asset_base_cost,
 167: 
 168:     productive_asset_allocated_overhead_cost,
 169:     support_asset_allocated_overhead_cost,
 170: 
 171:     productive_asset_recovery_cost,
 172:     support_asset_recovery_cost,
 173: 
 174:     productive_asset_cost: productive_asset_recovery_cost,
 175:     support_asset_cost: support_asset_recovery_cost,
 176: 
 177:     total_allocated_asset_overhead_cost:
 178:       productive_asset_allocated_overhead_cost +
 179:       support_asset_allocated_overhead_cost,
 180: 
 181:     total_asset_recovery_cost:
 182:       productive_asset_recovery_cost + support_asset_recovery_cost,
 183:   };
 184: }
 185: 
 186: export function useCostAllocationAssetOverlay({
 187:   asset_output_contract = {},
 188:   recovery_hours_used = 0,
 189: }) {
 190:   return useMemo(() => {
 191:     return build_asset_recovery_overlay({
 192:       asset_output_contract,
 193:       recovery_hours_used,
 194:     });
 195:   }, [asset_output_contract, recovery_hours_used]);
 196: }
